SELECT MAX(`Percent (%) Eligible Free (K-12)`) AS max_eligible_free_rate FROM frpm INNER JOIN schools ON frpm.`CDSCode` = schools.`CDSCode` WHERE schools.`County` = 'Alameda';

WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT f.`CDSCode`, f.`Charter School (Y/N)`, s.Zip FROM frpm f INNER JOIN schools s ON f.`CDSCode` = s.`CDSCode` WHERE f.`Charter School (Y/N)` = 1 AND s.`District` = 'Fresno County Office of Education' ) SELECT DISTINCT Zip FROM c_cols;
SELECT `MailStreet` FROM ( SELECT f.`CDSCode`, f.`FRPM Count (K-12)`, s.`MailStreet` FROM frpm f INNER JOIN schools s ON f.`CDSCode` = s.`CDSCode` ORDER BY `FRPM Count (K-12)` DESC LIMIT 1 ) AS final_answer;
SELECT s.`Phone` FROM schools AS s INNER JOIN frpm AS f ON s.`CDSCode` = f.`CDSCode` WHERE s.`OpenDate` > '2000-01-01' AND f.`Charter School (Y/N)` = 1 AND s.`Phone` IS NOT NULL;
SELECT COUNT(*) AS answer FROM satscores ss JOIN schools s ON ss.cds = s.`CDSCode` WHERE ss.`AvgScrMath` > 400 AND s.`Virtual` = 'F';
SELECT s.`School` FROM schools s JOIN satscores ss ON s.`CDSCode` = ss.`cds` WHERE ss.`NumTstTakr` > 500 AND s.`Magnet` = 1;
WITH school_test_takers AS ( SELECT s.`CDSCode`, SUM(ss.NumGE1500) AS total_test_takers FROM satscores AS ss INNER JOIN schools AS s ON ss.cds = s.`CDSCode` WHERE ss.NumGE1500 > 0 GROUP BY s.`CDSCode` ), top_school AS ( SELECT `CDSCode` FROM school_test_takers ORDER BY total_test_takers DESC LIMIT 1 ) SELECT `Phone` FROM schools WHERE `CDSCode` = (SELECT `CDSCode` FROM top_school);
WITH top_frpm_school AS ( SELECT `CDSCode` FROM frpm ORDER BY `FRPM Count (K-12)` DESC LIMIT 1 ) SELECT ss.`NumTstTakr` FROM schools AS s INNER JOIN satscores AS ss ON s.`CDSCode` = ss.`cds` WHERE s.`CDSCode` = (SELECT `CDSCode` FROM top_frpm_school);
SELECT COUNT(*) AS answer FROM satscores JOIN frpm ON satscores.cds = frpm.`CDSCode` WHERE satscores.`AvgScrMath` > 560 AND frpm.`Charter Funding Type` = 'Directly funded';
SELECT f.`FRPM Count (Ages 5-17)` FROM satscores ss JOIN frpm f ON ss.cds = f.`CDSCode` WHERE ss.`AvgScrRead` IS NOT NULL ORDER BY ss.`AvgScrRead` DESC LIMIT 1;
SELECT `CDSCode` FROM frpm WHERE `Enrollment (K-12)` + `Enrollment (Ages 5-17)` > 500;
WITH filtered_schools AS ( SELECT f.`Percent (%) Eligible Free (Ages 5-17)` FROM frpm f INNER JOIN satscores s ON f.`CDSCode` = s.cds WHERE s.`NumTstTakr` > 0 AND s.`NumGE1500` IS NOT NULL AND (s.`NumGE1500` / s.`NumTstTakr`) > 0.3 ) SELECT MAX(`Percent (%) Eligible Free (Ages 5-17)`) AS answer FROM filtered_schools;
WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT ss.cds, ss.NumTstTakr, ss.NumGE1500, sc.Phone FROM satscores AS ss JOIN schools AS sc ON ss.cds = sc.CDSCode WHERE ss.NumTstTakr > 0 ), c_calculate AS ( SELECT cds, NumTstTakr, NumGE1500, Phone, (NumGE1500 / NumTstTakr) * 100 AS ExcellenceRate FROM c_cols WHERE NumGE1500 IS NOT NULL AND NumTstTakr > 0 ), c_rank AS ( SELECT cds, Phone, ExcellenceRate, RANK() OVER (ORDER BY ExcellenceRate DESC) AS Rank FROM c_calculate ), c_top3 AS ( SELECT cds, Phone FROM c_rank WHERE Rank <= 3 ) SELECT Phone FROM c_top3 LIMIT 3;

WITH final_answer AS ( SELECT `District`, MAX(`AvgScrRead`) AS max_avg_read FROM ( SELECT s.`District`, ss.`AvgScrRead` FROM satscores ss INNER JOIN schools s ON ss.cds = s.`CDSCode` WHERE ss.`AvgScrRead` IS NOT NULL AND s.`StatusType` = 'Active' ) GROUP BY `District` ORDER BY max_avg_read DESC LIMIT 1 ) SELECT `District` FROM final_answer;
SELECT COUNT(*) AS count FROM schools AS s INNER JOIN satscores AS ss ON s.`CDSCode` = ss.`cds` WHERE (s.`County` LIKE '%Alameda%' OR s.`District` LIKE '%Alameda%' OR s.`School` LIKE '%Alameda%' OR s.`Street` LIKE '%Alameda%' OR s.`City` LIKE '%Alameda%' OR s.`Zip` LIKE '%Alameda%' OR s.`State` LIKE '%Alameda%' OR s.`MailCity` LIKE '%Alameda%' OR s.`MailZip` LIKE '%Alameda%' OR s.`MailState` LIKE '%Alameda%') AND ss.`NumTstTakr` < 100;
SELECT s.`CDSCode`, s.`CharterNum`, ss.`AvgScrWrite` FROM satscores ss INNER JOIN schools s ON ss.cds = s.`CDSCode` WHERE ss.`AvgScrWrite` > 499 AND ss.`AvgScrWrite` IS NOT NULL AND s.`CharterNum` IS NOT NULL ORDER BY ss.`AvgScrWrite` DESC;
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT schools.CDSCode) FROM schools JOIN satscores ON schools.CDSCode = satscores.cds WHERE schools.County = 'Fresno' AND schools.FundingType = 'Directly Funded' AND satscores.NumTstTakr <= 250;
WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT ss.cds, ss.`AvgScrMath`, sc.Phone FROM satscores AS ss INNER JOIN schools AS sc ON ss.cds = sc.`CDSCode` ), c_rows AS ( SELECT cds, `AvgScrMath`, Phone FROM c_cols WHERE `AvgScrMath` IS NOT NULL ), t_rank AS ( SELECT cds, `AvgScrMath`, Phone, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY `AvgScrMath` DESC) AS rn FROM c_rows ), t_top AS ( SELECT cds, Phone FROM t_rank WHERE rn = 1 ) SELECT Phone FROM t_top;
SELECT COUNT(*) AS answer FROM ( SELECT s.`CDSCode` FROM frpm f INNER JOIN schools s ON f.`CDSCode` = s.`CDSCode` WHERE f.`Low Grade` = '9' AND f.`High Grade` = '12' AND s.`County` = 'Amador' ) AS t_join;
SELECT COUNT(*) AS answer FROM frpm INNER JOIN schools ON frpm.`CDSCode` = schools.`CDSCode` WHERE schools.`County` = 'Los Angeles' AND frpm.`Free Meal Count (K-12)` > 500 AND frpm.`FRPM Count (K-12)` < 700;

SELECT schools.School, schools.Street FROM schools JOIN frpm ON schools.CDSCode = frpm.CDSCode WHERE ABS(`Enrollment (K-12)` - `Enrollment (Ages 5-17)`) > 30;
WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT f.`School Name`, f.`Percent (%) Eligible Free (K-12)`, ss.`NumGE1500` FROM frpm AS f INNER JOIN satscores AS ss ON f.`CDSCode` = ss.cds ) SELECT `School Name` FROM c_cols WHERE `Percent (%) Eligible Free (K-12)` > 0.1 AND `NumGE1500` > 0;
SELECT sc.`School`, sc.`FundingType` FROM satscores ss JOIN schools sc ON ss.cds = sc.`CDSCode` WHERE sc.`County` = 'Riverside' AND ss.`AvgScrMath` > 400;

WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT s.`School`, s.`CDSCode`, ss.`AvgScrWrite`, s.`Phone`, s.`OpenDate`, s.`ClosedDate` FROM schools AS s LEFT JOIN satscores AS ss ON s.`CDSCode` = ss.`cds` ) SELECT `School`, `AvgScrWrite`, `Phone` FROM c_cols WHERE (`OpenDate` > '1991-12-31' OR (`ClosedDate` IS NOT NULL AND `ClosedDate` < '2000-12-31')) AND `AvgScrWrite` IS NOT NULL ORDER BY `School`;
WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT f.`CDSCode`, f.`Enrollment (K-12)`, f.`Enrollment (Ages 5-17)`, s.`School`, s.`DOC`, s.`DOCType` FROM frpm AS f INNER JOIN schools AS s ON f.`CDSCode` = s.`CDSCode` WHERE f.`Charter Funding Type` = 'Locally funded' ), c_diff AS ( SELECT `CDSCode`, `Enrollment (K-12)` - `Enrollment (Ages 5-17)` AS `Difference`, `School`, `DOC`, `DOCType` FROM c_cols ), c_avg_diff AS ( SELECT AVG(`Difference`) AS `AverageDifference` FROM c_diff ), c_above_avg AS ( SELECT `CDSCode`, `School`, `DOC`, `DOCType` FROM c_diff WHERE `Difference` > (SELECT `AverageDifference` FROM c_avg_diff) ) SELECT `School`, `DOCType` FROM c_above_avg;
WITH k12_schools AS ( SELECT f.`School Name`, f.`Enrollment (K-12)`, s.`OpenDate` FROM frpm AS f JOIN schools AS s ON f.`CDSCode` = s.`CDSCode` WHERE f.`Low Grade` = 'K' AND f.`High Grade` = '12' ) SELECT `OpenDate` FROM k12_schools ORDER BY `Enrollment (K-12)` DESC LIMIT 1;
SELECT `City`, SUM(`Enrollment (K-12)`) AS total_enrollment FROM schools JOIN frpm ON schools.CDSCode = frpm.CDSCode GROUP BY `City` ORDER BY total_enrollment ASC LIMIT 5;
WITH ranked_schools AS ( SELECT `School Code`, `Enrollment (K-12)`, `Percent (%) Eligible Free (K-12)`, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY `Enrollment (K-12)` DESC) AS rank FROM frpm ) SELECT `Percent (%) Eligible Free (K-12)` FROM ranked_schools WHERE rank BETWEEN 10 AND 11;
WITH filtered_schools AS ( SELECT f.`CDSCode`, f.`FRPM Count (K-12)`, f.`Enrollment (K-12)` FROM frpm AS f INNER JOIN schools AS s ON f.`CDSCode` = s.`CDSCode` WHERE s.`Charter` = 1 AND f.`Enrollment (K-12)` IS NOT NULL AND f.`Enrollment (K-12)` > 0 AND f.`FRPM Count (K-12)` IS NOT NULL ), top_schools AS ( SELECT `CDSCode`, `FRPM Count (K-12)`, `Enrollment (K-12)` FROM filtered_schools ORDER BY `FRPM Count (K-12)` DESC LIMIT 5 ) SELECT (`FRPM Count (K-12)` / `Enrollment (K-12)`) * 100 AS `Eligible FRPM Rate (K-12)` FROM top_schools;
SELECT s.`School`, s.`Website` FROM schools s JOIN frpm f ON s.`School` = f.`School Name` WHERE f.`Free Meal Count (Ages 5-17)` BETWEEN 1900 AND 2000 AND s.`Website` IS NOT NULL;
WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT f.`Free Meal Count (Ages 5-17)`, f.`Enrollment (Ages 5-17)` FROM frpm AS f INNER JOIN schools AS s ON f.`CDSCode` = s.`CDSCode` WHERE s.`AdmFName1` = 'Kacey' AND s.`AdmLName1` = 'Gibson' ), t_agg AS ( SELECT `Free Meal Count (Ages 5-17)` / `Enrollment (Ages 5-17)` AS free_rate FROM c_cols ) SELECT free_rate FROM t_agg;

WITH school_scores AS ( SELECT s.CDSCode, s.School, ss.NumGE1500 FROM satscores ss JOIN schools s ON ss.cds = s.CDSCode WHERE ss.NumGE1500 > 0 ), top_school AS ( SELECT CDSCode FROM school_scores ORDER BY NumGE1500 DESC LIMIT 1 ) SELECT `AdmFName1`, `AdmLName1`, `AdmFName2`, `AdmLName2`, `AdmFName3`, `AdmLName3` FROM schools WHERE CDSCode = (SELECT CDSCode FROM top_school);

WITH filtered_schools AS ( SELECT s.`CDSCode`, s.`OpenDate`, ss.`NumTstTakr` FROM schools AS s LEFT JOIN satscores AS ss ON s.`CDSCode` = ss.`cds` WHERE s.`OpenDate` BETWEEN '1980-01-01' AND '1980-12-31' AND s.`County` = 'Fresno' AND ss.`NumTstTakr` IS NOT NULL ) SELECT AVG(`NumTstTakr`) AS answer FROM filtered_schools;
SELECT s.`Phone` FROM satscores ss JOIN schools s ON ss.cds = s.`CDSCode` WHERE s.`District` = 'Fresno Unified' AND ss.`AvgScrRead` IS NOT NULL ORDER BY ss.`AvgScrRead` ASC LIMIT 1;
WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT ss.cds, ss.`AvgScrRead`, f.`County Name`, f.`School Name`, sc.`Virtual` FROM satscores AS ss INNER JOIN frpm AS f ON ss.cds = f.`CDSCode` INNER JOIN schools AS sc ON ss.cds = sc.`CDSCode` WHERE sc.`Virtual` = 'F' ), c_rows AS ( SELECT cds, `AvgScrRead`, `County Name`, `School Name` FROM c_cols WHERE `AvgScrRead` IS NOT NULL ), t_rank AS ( SELECT cds, `AvgScrRead`, `County Name`, `School Name`, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY `County Name` ORDER BY `AvgScrRead` DESC) AS rank FROM c_rows ) SELECT DISTINCT `School Name` FROM t_rank WHERE rank <= 5;
SELECT sc.`GSoffered` FROM satscores ss JOIN schools sc ON ss.cds = sc.`CDSCode` WHERE ss.`AvgScrMath` IS NOT NULL ORDER BY ss.`AvgScrMath` DESC LIMIT 1;
WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT s.cds, s.`AvgScrMath`, s.`AvgScrRead`, s.`AvgScrWrite`, sc.`County` FROM satscores AS s JOIN schools AS sc ON s.cds = sc.`CDSCode` ), c_rows AS ( SELECT cds, `AvgScrMath`, `AvgScrRead`, `AvgScrWrite`, `County` FROM c_cols WHERE `AvgScrMath` IS NOT NULL AND `AvgScrRead` IS NOT NULL AND `AvgScrWrite` IS NOT NULL ), t_agg AS ( SELECT cds, `AvgScrMath`, `AvgScrRead`, `AvgScrWrite`, `County`, `AvgScrMath` + `AvgScrRead` + `AvgScrWrite` AS total_avg_score FROM c_rows ), t_order AS ( SELECT cds, `AvgScrMath`, `County` FROM t_agg ORDER BY total_avg_score ASC LIMIT 1 ) SELECT `AvgScrMath`, `County` FROM t_order;
SELECT s.AvgScrWrite, sc.City FROM satscores s JOIN schools sc ON s.cds = sc.CDSCode WHERE s.NumGE1500 = (SELECT MAX(NumGE1500) FROM satscores WHERE NumGE1500 IS NOT NULL AND NumGE1500 > 0);
SELECT `School Code`, `School Name`, AVG(`AvgScrWrite`) AS `AvgScrWrite` FROM ( SELECT f.`School Code`, f.`School Name`, ss.`AvgScrWrite` FROM frpm f INNER JOIN schools s ON f.`CDSCode` = s.`CDSCode` INNER JOIN satscores ss ON s.`CDSCode` = ss.`cds` WHERE s.`AdmFName1` = 'Ricci' AND s.`AdmLName1` = 'Ulrich' ) AS c_cols GROUP BY `School Code`, `School Name`;
SELECT s.`State`, f.`Enrollment (K-12)` FROM frpm AS f INNER JOIN schools AS s ON f.`CDSCode` = s.`CDSCode` WHERE s.`DOC` = '31' AND f.`Enrollment (K-12)` = ( SELECT MAX(`Enrollment (K-12)`) FROM frpm AS f2 INNER JOIN schools AS s2 ON f2.`CDSCode` = s2.`CDSCode` WHERE s2.`DOC` = '31' );
SELECT COUNT(*) / 12 AS monthly_avg FROM schools s JOIN frpm f ON s.`CDSCode` = f.`CDSCode` WHERE strftime('%Y', s.`OpenDate`) = '1980' AND f.`District Type` = 'Elementary School District' AND f.`County Name` = 'Alameda';
WITH c_union AS ( SELECT f.`CDSCode`, 'Unified' AS `District Type` FROM frpm AS f INNER JOIN schools AS s ON f.`CDSCode` = s.`CDSCode` WHERE s.`DOC` = '54' AND f.`County Name` = 'Orange County' UNION SELECT f.`CDSCode`, 'Elementary' AS `District Type` FROM frpm AS f INNER JOIN schools AS s ON f.`CDSCode` = s.`CDSCode` WHERE s.`DOC` = '52' AND f.`County Name` = 'Orange County' ), c_count AS ( SELECT SUM(CASE WHEN `District Type` = 'Unified' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS unified_count, SUM(CASE WHEN `District Type` = 'Elementary' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS elementary_count FROM c_union ) SELECT CAST(unified_count AS REAL) / CAST(elementary_count AS REAL) AS ratio FROM c_count;
WITH closed_schools AS ( SELECT `County`, `School`, `ClosedDate` FROM schools WHERE `StatusType` = 'Closed' ), county_counts AS ( SELECT `County`, COUNT(`School`) AS school_count FROM closed_schools GROUP BY `County` ), max_county AS ( SELECT `County` FROM county_counts ORDER BY school_count DESC LIMIT 1 ) SELECT `County`, `School`, `ClosedDate` FROM closed_schools WHERE `County` = (SELECT `County` FROM max_county) ORDER BY `County` ASC;
SELECT s.`School`, s.`MailStreet` FROM schools s JOIN satscores sc ON s.`CDSCode` = sc.`cds` ORDER BY sc.`AvgScrMath` DESC LIMIT 1 OFFSET 6;
WITH t_join AS ( SELECT s.`School`, s.`MailStreet`, ss.`AvgScrRead` FROM schools AS s INNER JOIN satscores AS ss ON s.`CDSCode` = ss.`cds` ), t_agg AS ( SELECT `School`, `MailStreet`, MIN(`AvgScrRead`) AS min_avg_read FROM t_join GROUP BY `School`, `MailStreet` ), t_filter AS ( SELECT `School`, `MailStreet` FROM t_agg WHERE min_avg_read = (SELECT MIN(min_avg_read) FROM t_agg) ) SELECT `School`, `MailStreet` FROM t_filter;

WITH filtered_loans AS ( SELECT a.account_id, l.amount FROM account a JOIN loan l ON a.account_id = l.account_id WHERE l.date BETWEEN '1997-01-01' AND '1997-12-31' ), weekly_accounts AS ( SELECT fl.account_id, fl.amount FROM filtered_loans fl JOIN account a ON fl.account_id = a.account_id WHERE a.frequency = 'POPLATEK TYDNE' ), min_amount AS ( SELECT MIN(amount) AS min_amt FROM weekly_accounts ) SELECT wa.account_id FROM weekly_accounts wa JOIN min_amount m ON wa.amount = m.min_amt;

WITH max_district AS ( SELECT district_id FROM district WHERE A3 LIKE '%south bohemia%' OR A3 LIKE '%south%bohemia%' OR A3 LIKE '%bohemia%' ORDER BY CAST(`A4` AS INTEGER) DESC LIMIT 1 ), client_counts AS ( SELECT COUNT(*) AS total_clients, SUM(CASE WHEN `gender` = 'M' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS male_clients FROM client WHERE district_id = (SELECT district_id FROM max_district) ) SELECT (male_clients * 100.0 / total_clients) AS percentage FROM client_counts;
WITH t_join AS ( SELECT l.account_id, l.date AS loan_date, t.date AS trans_date, t.balance FROM loan l INNER JOIN trans t ON l.account_id = t.account_id WHERE l.date = '1993-07-05' ), t_filtered AS ( SELECT account_id, balance AS balance_1993_03_22 FROM t_join WHERE trans_date = '1993-03-22' ), t_filtered2 AS ( SELECT account_id, balance AS balance_1998_12_27 FROM t_join WHERE trans_date = '1998-12-27' ), t_final AS ( SELECT f.account_id, f.balance_1993_03_22, t.balance_1998_12_27 FROM t_filtered f INNER JOIN t_filtered2 t ON f.account_id = t.account_id ) SELECT (balance_1998_12_27 - balance_1993_03_22) * 100.0 / balance_1993_03_22 AS increase_rate FROM t_final;

WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT d.district_id, d.A12, d.A13 FROM district d ), c_rows AS ( SELECT district_id, A12 AS unemployment_2015, A13 AS unemployment_2016 FROM c_cols WHERE A12 IS NOT NULL AND A13 IS NOT NULL ), t_unemployment AS ( SELECT district_id, unemployment_2015, unemployment_2016, ((unemployment_2016 - unemployment_2015) / unemployment_2015 * 100) AS unemployment_increment FROM c_rows ), t_loans AS ( SELECT DISTINCT l.account_id FROM loan l WHERE l.status = 'D' ), t_accounts AS ( SELECT DISTINCT a.district_id FROM t_loans l JOIN account a ON l.account_id = a.account_id ), t_districts AS ( SELECT u.district_id, u.unemployment_increment FROM t_unemployment u JOIN t_accounts a ON u.district_id = a.district_id ), t_final AS ( SELECT t.district_id, d.A2 AS district, d.A3 AS state, t.unemployment_increment FROM t_districts t JOIN district d ON t.district_id = d.district_id ), final_result AS ( SELECT district, state, unemployment_increment FROM t_final ) SELECT district, state, unemployment_increment FROM final_result;

WITH t_join AS ( SELECT c.atom_id, c.atom_id2, b.bond_id FROM connected c JOIN bond b ON c.bond_id = b.bond_id WHERE b.bond_type = '#' ), t_elements AS ( SELECT DISTINCT a1.element AS element1, a2.element AS element2 FROM t_join tj JOIN atom a1 ON tj.atom_id = a1.atom_id JOIN atom a2 ON tj.atom_id2 = a2.atom_id ), final_elements AS ( SELECT DISTINCT element1 AS element FROM t_elements WHERE element1 < element2 ) SELECT * FROM final_elements;

WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT a.atom_id, a.molecule_id, a.element, b.bond_type, c.atom_id2, c.bond_id, m.label FROM atom a JOIN bond b ON a.molecule_id = b.molecule_id JOIN connected c ON b.bond_id = c.bond_id JOIN molecule m ON a.molecule_id = m.molecule_id ), c_rows AS ( SELECT atom_id, molecule_id, element, bond_type, atom_id2, bond_id, label FROM c_cols WHERE bond_type = '-' ), t_join AS ( SELECT a.atom_id, a.element, a.molecule_id, a.label, c.atom_id2 FROM c_rows a JOIN connected c ON a.atom_id = c.atom_id ), t_atoms AS ( SELECT a.atom_id, a.element, a.molecule_id, m.label, c.atom_id2, a2.element AS connected_element FROM t_join a JOIN connected c ON a.atom_id = c.atom_id JOIN atom a2 ON c.atom_id2 = a2.atom_id JOIN molecule m ON a.molecule_id = m.molecule_id ), t_unique AS ( SELECT DISTINCT t.atom_id, t.element, t.molecule_id, t.label, t.atom_id2, t.connected_element FROM t_atoms t ), t_final AS ( SELECT t.molecule_id, t.label, t.element AS atom1, t.connected_element AS atom2 FROM t_unique t WHERE t.atom_id != t.atom_id2 ), t_filtered AS ( SELECT molecule_id, label, atom1, atom2 FROM t_final WHERE atom1 != atom2 ), t_result AS ( SELECT molecule_id, label, atom1, atom2 FROM t_filtered WHERE atom1 < atom2 ), t_distinct_bonds AS ( SELECT DISTINCT molecule_id, label, atom1, atom2 FROM t_result ) SELECT molecule_id, label, atom1, atom2 FROM t_distinct_bonds;
WITH triple_bond_molecules AS ( SELECT DISTINCT m.molecule_id FROM molecule m JOIN bond b ON m.molecule_id = b.molecule_id WHERE b.bond_type = '#' ), elements_per_molecule AS ( SELECT t.molecule_id, a.element FROM triple_bond_molecules t JOIN atom a ON t.molecule_id = a.molecule_id ) SELECT molecule_id, STRING_AGG(element, ', ') AS elements FROM elements_per_molecule GROUP BY molecule_id;

WITH final_output AS ( SELECT t.`superhero_name`, t.`publisher_name` FROM ( SELECT r.`superhero_name`, r.`publisher_name` FROM ( SELECT s.`superhero_name`, p.`publisher_name` FROM ( SELECT s.`superhero_name`, s.`publisher_id`, s.`eye_colour_id`, s.`hair_colour_id`, s.`skin_colour_id` FROM superhero AS s ) AS s JOIN publisher p ON s.publisher_id = p.id WHERE s.`eye_colour_id` = s.`hair_colour_id` AND s.`hair_colour_id` = s.`skin_colour_id` ) AS r JOIN superhero s ON r.`superhero_name` = s.`superhero_name` JOIN colour c ON s.`eye_colour_id` = c.id JOIN colour h ON s.`hair_colour_id` = h.id JOIN colour sk ON s.`skin_colour_id` = sk.id WHERE c.`colour` = h.`colour` AND h.`colour` = sk.`colour` ) AS t GROUP BY t.`superhero_name`, t.`publisher_name` HAVING COUNT(*) > 1 ) SELECT * FROM final_output;

WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT r.`fastestLapTime` FROM results AS r INNER JOIN drivers AS d ON r.`driverId` = d.`driverId` WHERE d.`forename` = 'Lewis' AND d.`surname` = 'Hamilton' ), c_rows AS ( SELECT `fastestLapTime` FROM c_cols WHERE `fastestLapTime` IS NOT NULL ), t_convert AS ( SELECT STRFTIME('%S', `fastestLapTime`, 'start=1970-01-01') * 1000 + SUBSTR(`fastestLapTime`, 2, INSTR(`fastestLapTime`, ':') - 1) * 1000 + SUBSTR(`fastestLapTime`, INSTR(`fastestLapTime`, ':') + 1, INSTR(`fastestLapTime`, '.') - INSTR(`fastestLapTime`, ':') - 1) AS `fastestLapTimeInSeconds` FROM c_rows ) SELECT AVG(`fastestLapTimeInSeconds`) AS average_fastest_lap_time FROM t_convert;
WITH race_info AS ( SELECT raceId FROM races WHERE year = 2008 AND name = 'Australian Grand Prix' ), driver_counts AS ( SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT driverId) AS total_drivers, COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN time IS NOT NULL THEN driverId END) AS completed_drivers FROM results WHERE raceId = (SELECT raceId FROM race_info) ) SELECT (completed_drivers * 1.0 / total_drivers) AS rate FROM driver_counts;
WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT r.`raceId`, r.`time` AS `champion_time`, r.`position` AS `champion_position` FROM results AS r WHERE r.`position` = 1 UNION ALL SELECT r.`raceId`, r.`time` AS `last_driver_time`, r.`position` AS `last_driver_position` FROM results AS r WHERE r.`raceId` = ( SELECT `raceId` FROM races WHERE `year` = 2008 AND `name` = 'Australian Grand Prix' ) AND r.`position` = ( SELECT MAX(`position`) FROM results WHERE `raceId` = ( SELECT `raceId` FROM races WHERE `year` = 2008 AND `name` = 'Australian Grand Prix' ) ) ), c_rows AS ( SELECT raceId, champion_time, (SELECT time FROM results WHERE raceId = c_cols.raceId AND position = (SELECT MAX(position) FROM results WHERE raceId = c_cols.raceId)) AS last_driver_time FROM c_cols ), t_convert_times AS ( SELECT raceId, CAST(champion_time AS REAL) AS champion_time_seconds, CAST(last_driver_time AS REAL) AS last_driver_time_seconds FROM c_rows ), t_fix_last_driver_time AS ( SELECT raceId, champion_time_seconds, (SELECT CAST(time AS REAL) FROM results WHERE raceId = t_convert_times.raceId AND position = (SELECT MAX(position) FROM results WHERE raceId = t_convert_times.raceId)) AS last_driver_time_seconds FROM t_convert_times ) SELECT ((last_driver_time_seconds - champion_time_seconds) / last_driver_time_seconds) * 100 AS percentage_faster FROM t_fix_last_driver_time WHERE raceId = ( SELECT `raceId` FROM races WHERE `year` = 2008 AND `name` = 'Australian Grand Prix' );

WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT c.constructorId, c.name, c.nationality, cr.raceId, cr.points FROM constructors AS c LEFT JOIN constructorResults AS cr ON c.constructorId = cr.constructorId ), c_rows AS ( SELECT constructorId, nationality, raceId, points FROM c_cols WHERE nationality = 'Japanese' AND points = 0 ), t_join AS ( SELECT DISTINCT cr.constructorId FROM c_rows cr JOIN races r ON cr.raceId = r.raceId GROUP BY cr.constructorId HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT r.raceId) = 2 ) SELECT COUNT(*) AS answer FROM t_join;

WITH c_rows AS ( SELECT l.`raceId`, l.`driverId`, l.`lap` FROM lapTimes AS l WHERE l.`lap` > 50 ) SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT r.constructorId) FROM c_rows cr JOIN results r ON cr.raceId = r.raceId AND cr.driverId = r.driverId JOIN constructors c ON r.constructorId = c.constructorId WHERE c.nationality = 'French';
SELECT (COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN r.time IS NOT NULL THEN d.driverId END) * 100.0 / COUNT(DISTINCT d.driverId)) AS completion_percentage FROM drivers d JOIN results r ON d.driverId = r.driverId JOIN races ra ON r.raceId = ra.raceId WHERE ra.year BETWEEN 2007 AND 2009 AND d.nationality = 'Japanese';
WITH champion_times AS ( SELECT r.year, res.time FROM races r JOIN results res ON r.raceId = res.raceId JOIN driverStandings ds ON res.driverId = ds.driverId WHERE ds.position = 1 AND r.year < 1975 AND res.time IS NOT NULL ) SELECT year, AVG( CAST(SUBSTR(time, 1, 2) AS INTEGER) * 3600 + CAST(SUBSTR(time, 4, 2) AS INTEGER) * 60 + CAST(SUBSTR(time, 7, 2) AS INTEGER) + CAST(SUBSTR(time, 10, 3) AS REAL) / 1000 ) AS avg_time_seconds FROM champion_times GROUP BY year;
SELECT DISTINCT d.forename, d.surname FROM drivers AS d INNER JOIN driverStandings AS ds ON d.driverId = ds.driverId WHERE strftime('%Y', d.dob) > '1975' AND ds.position = 2;
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT r.driverId) AS answer FROM results AS r INNER JOIN drivers AS d ON r.driverId = d.driverId WHERE d.nationality = 'Italian' AND r.time IS NULL;
SELECT forename, surname FROM ( SELECT l.driverId, d.forename, d.surname, l.time FROM lapTimes AS l JOIN drivers AS d ON l.driverId = d.driverId ORDER BY l.time ASC LIMIT 1 );
SELECT `fastestLap` FROM results INNER JOIN races ON results.`raceId` = races.`raceId` INNER JOIN drivers ON results.`driverId` = drivers.`driverId` WHERE races.`year` = 2009 AND results.`position` = 1 ORDER BY results.`fastestLap` ASC LIMIT 1;
SELECT AVG(`fastestLapSpeed`) AS answer FROM results JOIN races ON results.raceId = races.raceId WHERE races.name = 'Spanish Grand Prix' AND races.year = 2009;
SELECT r.year, r.name FROM races AS r JOIN lapTimes AS l ON r.raceId = l.raceId WHERE l.milliseconds IS NOT NULL ORDER BY l.milliseconds ASC LIMIT 1;

SELECT d.code FROM drivers d WHERE d.nationality LIKE '%America%' OR d.nationality LIKE '%American%' OR d.nationality LIKE '%USA%' OR d.nationality LIKE '%United States%';
SELECT raceId FROM races WHERE year = 2009;
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT driverId) AS answer FROM results WHERE raceId = 18;
WITH top_youngest AS ( SELECT driverId, code, nationality FROM drivers ORDER BY dob ASC LIMIT 3 ) SELECT code FROM top_youngest UNION ALL SELECT (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM top_youngest WHERE nationality IN ('Dutch', 'Netherlandic')) AS netherlandic_count;
SELECT driverRef FROM drivers WHERE forename = 'Robert' AND surname = 'Kubica';
SELECT COUNT(*) AS answer FROM drivers WHERE nationality = 'British' AND STRFTIME('%Y', dob) = '1980';
WITH filtered_drivers AS ( SELECT d.driverId, d.forename, d.surname, d.dob, l.time FROM drivers AS d JOIN lapTimes AS l ON d.driverId = l.driverId WHERE d.nationality = 'German' AND strftime('%Y', d.dob) BETWEEN '1980' AND '1990' AND l.time IS NOT NULL ) SELECT forename, surname, time FROM filtered_drivers ORDER BY time ASC LIMIT 3;

SELECT d.driverId, d.code FROM drivers d JOIN results r ON d.driverId = r.driverId WHERE strftime('%Y', d.dob) = '1971' AND r.fastestLapTime IS NOT NULL ORDER BY r.fastestLapTime ASC LIMIT 1;
SELECT forename, surname, MAX(l.time) AS latest_time FROM drivers AS d INNER JOIN lapTimes AS l ON d.driverId = l.driverId WHERE d.nationality = 'Spanish' AND strftime('%Y', d.dob) < '1982' GROUP BY d.driverId ORDER BY latest_time DESC LIMIT 10;
SELECT r.year FROM results AS res JOIN races AS r ON res.raceId = r.raceId ORDER BY res.fastestLapTime ASC LIMIT 1;
SELECT r.`year` FROM lapTimes lt INNER JOIN races r ON lt.`raceId` = r.`raceId` ORDER BY lt.`time` DESC LIMIT 1;
SELECT `driverId` FROM ( SELECT `driverId`, MIN(`time`) AS `min_time` FROM lapTimes WHERE `lap` = 1 AND `time` IS NOT NULL GROUP BY `driverId` ORDER BY `min_time` ASC LIMIT 5 );
WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT r.raceId, r.time, rs.statusId FROM races AS r LEFT JOIN results AS rs ON r.raceId = rs.raceId ), c_rows AS ( SELECT raceId FROM c_cols WHERE raceId > 50 AND raceId < 100 AND time IS NOT NULL AND statusId = 2 ), t_distinct AS ( SELECT DISTINCT raceId FROM c_rows ) SELECT COUNT(*) AS answer FROM t_distinct;
SELECT COUNT(*) AS count, `location`, `lat`, `lng` FROM circuits WHERE `country` = 'Austria' GROUP BY `location`, `lat`, `lng`;
WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT r.`raceId`, r.`name`, COUNT(DISTINCT rs.`driverId`) AS finishers FROM races AS r LEFT JOIN results AS rs ON r.`raceId` = rs.`raceId` WHERE rs.`time` IS NOT NULL GROUP BY r.`raceId`, r.`name` ), c_rows AS ( SELECT `raceId`, `name`, `finishers` FROM c_cols ORDER BY `finishers` DESC LIMIT 1 ) SELECT * FROM c_rows;
SELECT d.driverRef, d.nationality, d.dob FROM qualifying AS q JOIN drivers AS d ON q.driverId = d.driverId WHERE q.raceId = 23 AND q.q2 IS NOT NULL;
WITH youngest_race_details AS ( SELECT r.year, d.forename, d.surname, r.date, r.time FROM drivers AS d INNER JOIN results AS res ON d.driverId = res.driverId INNER JOIN races AS r ON res.raceId = r.raceId WHERE res.statusId = 1 ORDER BY d.dob DESC LIMIT 1 ) SELECT * FROM youngest_race_details;

WITH t_join AS ( SELECT c.constructorId, c.name, c.url, cr.points FROM constructors AS c INNER JOIN constructorResults AS cr ON c.constructorId = cr.constructorId WHERE c.nationality = 'Italian' ), t_agg AS ( SELECT constructorId, MAX(points) AS max_points FROM t_join GROUP BY constructorId ), t_final AS ( SELECT t.constructorId, c.name, c.url FROM t_agg t JOIN constructors c ON t.constructorId = c.constructorId WHERE t.max_points = (SELECT MAX(max_points) FROM t_agg) ) SELECT name, url FROM t_final;
WITH c_rows AS ( SELECT cs.constructorId, cs.url, csr.wins FROM constructors cs LEFT JOIN constructorStandings csr ON cs.constructorId = csr.constructorId ), c_agg AS ( SELECT constructorId, MAX(wins) AS max_wins FROM c_rows GROUP BY constructorId ), c_max AS ( SELECT constructorId FROM c_agg ORDER BY max_wins DESC LIMIT 1 ) SELECT url FROM constructors WHERE constructorId = (SELECT constructorId FROM c_max);
SELECT d.forename, d.surname FROM lapTimes l JOIN races r ON l.raceId = r.raceId JOIN drivers d ON l.driverId = d.driverId WHERE r.name = 'French Grand Prix' AND l.lap = 3 ORDER BY l.time DESC LIMIT 1;
WITH lap_first_laps AS ( SELECT r.raceId, r.name, l.milliseconds FROM races AS r INNER JOIN lapTimes AS l ON r.raceId = l.raceId WHERE l.lap = 1 ), min_milliseconds_per_race AS ( SELECT raceId, MIN(milliseconds) AS min_milliseconds FROM lap_first_laps GROUP BY raceId ), final_result AS ( SELECT r.name, m.min_milliseconds FROM min_milliseconds_per_race AS m INNER JOIN races AS r ON m.raceId = r.raceId WHERE m.min_milliseconds IS NOT NULL ORDER BY m.min_milliseconds ASC LIMIT 1 ) SELECT * FROM final_result;
WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT r.`raceId`, rs.`fastestLapTime`, rs.`rank` FROM races AS r INNER JOIN results AS rs ON r.`raceId` = rs.`raceId` WHERE r.`year` = 2006 AND r.`name` = 'United States Grand Prix' ), c_rows AS ( SELECT `fastestLapTime` FROM c_cols WHERE `rank` < 11 ) SELECT AVG(`fastestLapTime`) AS average_fastest_lap_time FROM c_rows;
WITH c_avg AS ( SELECT d.driverId, d.forename, d.surname, d.dob, AVG(p.duration) AS avg_duration FROM drivers AS d LEFT JOIN pitStops AS p ON d.driverId = p.driverId WHERE d.nationality = 'German' AND d.dob > '1980-01-01' AND d.dob < '1985-01-01' GROUP BY d.driverId, d.forename, d.surname, d.dob ) SELECT forename, surname FROM c_avg WHERE avg_duration IS NOT NULL ORDER BY avg_duration ASC LIMIT 3;
WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT r.driverId, d.forename, d.surname, r.time FROM results AS r INNER JOIN drivers AS d ON r.driverId = d.driverId INNER JOIN races AS ra ON r.raceId = ra.raceId WHERE ra.name = 'Canadian Grand Prix' AND ra.year = 2008 ), c_rows AS ( SELECT driverId, forename, surname, time FROM c_cols WHERE time LIKE '1:36:24.227' ) SELECT forename, surname, time FROM c_rows;
WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT r.`raceId`, r.`year`, r.`name`, r.`date`, r.`time`, r.`url`, cs.`constructorId`, cs.`points`, cs.`status`, co.`constructorRef`, co.`name`, co.`url` FROM races AS r INNER JOIN constructorResults AS cs ON r.`raceId` = cs.`raceId` INNER JOIN constructors AS co ON cs.`constructorId` = co.`constructorId` WHERE r.`name` = 'Singapore Grand Prix' AND r.`year` = 2009 ) SELECT `constructorRef`, `url` FROM c_cols ORDER BY `points` DESC LIMIT 1;

WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT d.driverId, d.forename, d.surname, r.points FROM drivers AS d JOIN results AS r ON d.driverId = r.driverId JOIN races AS rc ON r.raceId = rc.raceId JOIN circuits AS c ON rc.circuitId = c.circuitId WHERE c.country = 'Turkey' ), c_rows AS ( SELECT driverId, points FROM c_cols WHERE forename = 'Lewis' AND surname = 'Hamilton' ) SELECT AVG(points) AS average_score FROM c_rows;

WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT l.`raceId`, l.`driverId`, l.`lap`, l.`time`, l.`milliseconds` FROM lapTimes AS l INNER JOIN drivers AS d ON l.`driverId` = d.`driverId` WHERE d.`forename` = 'Lewis' AND d.`surname` = 'Hamilton' ), c_rows AS ( SELECT `raceId`, `driverId`, `lap`, `time`, `milliseconds` FROM c_cols ORDER BY `milliseconds` ASC LIMIT 1 ) SELECT `time` AS answer FROM c_rows;
WITH t_final_answer AS ( SELECT d.forename, d.surname FROM lapTimes AS l JOIN drivers AS d ON l.driverId = d.driverId WHERE l.time = ( SELECT MIN(time) FROM lapTimes ) ORDER BY d.forename, d.surname LIMIT 20 ) SELECT * FROM t_final_answer;
WITH driver_id AS ( SELECT `driverId` FROM `drivers` WHERE `forename` = 'Lewis' AND `surname` = 'Hamilton' ), fastest_lap AS ( SELECT r.`raceId` FROM `results` AS r INNER JOIN `lapTimes` AS l ON r.`raceId` = l.`raceId` AND r.`driverId` = l.`driverId` WHERE r.`driverId` = (SELECT `driverId` FROM driver_id) AND r.`fastestLapTime` = ( SELECT MIN(`fastestLapTime`) FROM `results` WHERE `driverId` = (SELECT `driverId` FROM driver_id) ) ), race_circuit AS ( SELECT r.`raceId`, c.`circuitId` FROM `races` AS r INNER JOIN `circuits` AS c ON r.`circuitId` = c.`circuitId` WHERE r.`raceId` = (SELECT `raceId` FROM fastest_lap) ) SELECT c.`name` AS `circuit_name` FROM `race_circuit` AS rc INNER JOIN `circuits` AS c ON rc.`circuitId` = c.`circuitId`;

WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT m.league_id, m.season, m.home_team_goal, m.away_team_goal FROM Match AS m ), c_rows AS ( SELECT league_id, season, home_team_goal, away_team_goal FROM c_cols WHERE season = '2015/2016' ), c_draws AS ( SELECT league_id, COUNT(*) AS draw_count FROM c_rows WHERE home_team_goal = away_team_goal GROUP BY league_id ), c_max AS ( SELECT league_id, draw_count FROM c_draws ORDER BY draw_count DESC LIMIT 1 ) SELECT l.name FROM c_max m INNER JOIN League l ON m.league_id = l.id;
WITH c_rows AS ( SELECT p.`player_api_id`, p.`birthday` FROM Player p JOIN Player_Attributes pa ON p.`player_api_id` = pa.`player_api_id` WHERE pa.`sprint_speed` >= 97 AND strftime('%Y', pa.`date`) BETWEEN '2013' AND '2015' ), c_age_calculated AS ( SELECT `player_api_id`, SUBSTR(julianday(DATETIME()) - julianday(`birthday`), 1, 2) AS `age` FROM c_rows ) SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT `player_api_id`) AS `answer` FROM c_age_calculated;
WITH c_rows AS ( SELECT league_id, COUNT(id) AS match_count FROM Match GROUP BY league_id ), t_league_max AS ( SELECT league_id, match_count FROM c_rows ORDER BY match_count DESC LIMIT 1 ) SELECT l.name AS league_name, t.match_count AS total_matches FROM t_league_max t INNER JOIN League l ON t.league_id = l.id;

SELECT `team_long_name` FROM Team JOIN Team_Attributes ON Team.team_api_id = Team_Attributes.team_api_id WHERE strftime('%Y', Team_Attributes.date) = '2012' AND Team_Attributes.buildUpPlayPassing IS NOT NULL AND Team_Attributes.buildUpPlayPassing > ( SELECT AVG(buildUpPlayPassing) FROM Team_Attributes WHERE strftime('%Y', date) = '2012' AND buildUpPlayPassing IS NOT NULL );
WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT p.`player_api_id`, p.`birthday`, pa.`preferred_foot` FROM Player p JOIN Player_Attributes pa ON p.`player_api_id` = pa.`player_api_id` ), c_rows AS ( SELECT `player_api_id`, `birthday`, `preferred_foot` FROM c_cols WHERE `preferred_foot` = 'left' AND STRFTIME('%Y', `birthday`) BETWEEN '1987' AND '1992' ), c_count AS ( SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT `player_api_id`) AS left_foot_count, COUNT(*) AS total_players FROM c_rows ), c_percentage AS ( SELECT ROUND((CAST(left_foot_count AS REAL) / total_players) * 100, 2) AS percentage FROM c_count ) SELECT * FROM c_percentage;
WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT m.league_id, SUM(m.home_team_goal + m.away_team_goal) AS total_goals FROM Match AS m GROUP BY m.league_id ), c_rows AS ( SELECT league_id, total_goals FROM c_cols ORDER BY total_goals ASC LIMIT 5 ), t_league_names AS ( SELECT l.id AS league_id, l.name AS league_name FROM League AS l WHERE l.id IN (SELECT league_id FROM c_rows) ) SELECT t.league_name, c.total_goals FROM t_league_names t JOIN c_rows c ON t.league_id = c.league_id ORDER BY c.total_goals ASC;

WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT p.`height`, pa.`overall_rating` FROM Player p JOIN Player_Attributes pa ON p.`player_api_id` = pa.`player_api_id` ), t_count AS ( SELECT COUNT(*) AS total_players FROM Player ), t_count_under_180 AS ( SELECT COUNT(*) AS count_under_180 FROM c_cols WHERE `height` < 180 AND `overall_rating` > 70 ) SELECT (count_under_180 * 100.0 / total_players) AS percentage FROM t_count_under_180, t_count;
WITH c_rows AS ( SELECT p.ID, p.SEX, p.Admission FROM Patient p WHERE p.SEX = 'M' ), t_male_inpatient_outpatient AS ( SELECT SUM(CASE WHEN Admission = '+' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS inpatient_count, SUM(CASE WHEN Admission = '-' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS outpatient_count FROM c_rows ), t_percentage AS ( SELECT (CAST(inpatient_count AS REAL) / CAST(outpatient_count AS REAL)) * 100 AS percentage_deviation FROM t_male_inpatient_outpatient ) SELECT percentage_deviation FROM t_percentage;
WITH c_female_birth AS ( SELECT COUNT(*) AS female_count_after_1930 FROM Patient WHERE SEX = 'F' AND strftime('%Y', Birthday) > '1930' ), c_female_total AS ( SELECT COUNT(*) AS female_total FROM Patient WHERE SEX = 'F' ) SELECT CAST(c_female_birth.female_count_after_1930 * 100.0 / c_female_total.female_total AS REAL) AS percentage FROM c_female_birth, c_female_total;
WITH t_count AS ( SELECT COUNT(*) AS total_patients FROM Patient WHERE strftime('%Y', Birthday) BETWEEN '1930' AND '1940' ), t_inpatient AS ( SELECT COUNT(*) AS inpatient_count FROM Patient WHERE strftime('%Y', Birthday) BETWEEN '1930' AND '1940' AND Admission = '+' ) SELECT (inpatient_count * 100.0 / total_patients) AS percentage FROM t_count, t_inpatient;

WITH t_monthly_count AS ( SELECT STRFTIME('%Y-%m', l.Date) AS month_year, COUNT(*) AS count FROM Patient p INNER JOIN Laboratory l ON p.ID = l.ID WHERE p.SEX = 'M' AND l.Date BETWEEN '1998-01-01' AND '1998-12-31' GROUP BY STRFTIME('%Y-%m', l.Date) ) SELECT SUM(count) / 12 AS average_monthly FROM t_monthly_count;
WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT p.`ID`, p.`Birthday`, p.`First Date`, l.`Date` FROM Patient p INNER JOIN Laboratory l ON p.`ID` = l.`ID` WHERE p.`Diagnosis` = 'SJS' ), c_rows AS ( SELECT ID, Birthday, MIN(`Date`) AS `Oldest Lab Date` FROM c_cols GROUP BY ID, Birthday ), t_join AS ( SELECT r.ID, r.Birthday, r.`Oldest Lab Date`, p.`First Date` FROM c_rows r INNER JOIN Patient p ON r.ID = p.`ID` ), t_age AS ( SELECT ID, Birthday, `Oldest Lab Date`, `First Date` FROM t_join WHERE `First Date` IS NOT NULL ) SELECT `Oldest Lab Date`, (STRFTIME('%Y', `First Date`) - STRFTIME('%Y', `Birthday`)) AS AgeAtArrival FROM t_age ORDER BY `Birthday` ASC LIMIT 1;

WITH t_filtered AS ( SELECT COUNT(*) AS total_female, COUNT(CASE WHEN UA > 6.5 THEN 1 END) AS female_over_6_5 FROM ( SELECT p.ID, p.SEX, l.UA FROM Patient p JOIN Laboratory l ON p.ID = l.ID WHERE p.SEX = 'F' AND l.UA IS NOT NULL ) ) SELECT (female_over_6_5 * 100.0 / total_female) AS percentage FROM t_filtered;
WITH latest_labs AS ( SELECT p.ID, l.UA, l.Date, p.SEX FROM Patient p JOIN Laboratory l ON p.ID = l.ID WHERE l.Date = ( SELECT MAX(Date) FROM Laboratory l2 WHERE l2.ID = p.ID ) ), normal_ua AS ( SELECT ID, UA FROM latest_labs WHERE (UA < 8.0 AND SEX = 'M') OR (UA < 6.5 AND SEX = 'F') ) SELECT AVG(UA) AS average_ua FROM normal_ua;
SELECT DISTINCT p.`ID`, p.`SEX`, p.`Birthday` FROM Patient AS p INNER JOIN Laboratory AS l ON p.`ID` = l.`ID` WHERE l.`UN` = 29.0;

SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS answer FROM Patient AS p INNER JOIN Laboratory AS l ON p.ID = l.ID WHERE p.SEX = 'M' AND l.CRE >= 1.5;
WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT p.ID, p.SEX, l.CRE FROM Patient AS p INNER JOIN Laboratory AS l ON p.ID = l.ID WHERE l.CRE >= 1.5 ), t_agg AS ( SELECT SUM(CASE WHEN SEX = 'M' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS male_count, SUM(CASE WHEN SEX = 'F' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS female_count FROM c_cols ) SELECT CASE WHEN male_count > female_count THEN 'True' ELSE 'False' END AS result FROM t_agg;
SELECT p.ID, p.SEX, p.Birthday FROM Patient p INNER JOIN Laboratory l ON p.ID = l.ID WHERE l.`T-BIL` = (SELECT MAX(`T-BIL`) FROM Laboratory WHERE `T-BIL` IS NOT NULL);
WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT p.ID, p.SEX, l.`T-BIL` FROM Patient AS p INNER JOIN Laboratory AS l ON p.ID = l.ID ), filtered_data AS ( SELECT ID, SEX FROM c_cols WHERE `T-BIL` IS NOT NULL AND `T-BIL` >= 2.0 ), t_group AS ( SELECT SEX, GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT ID) AS patient_ids FROM filtered_data GROUP BY SEX ) SELECT SEX, patient_ids FROM t_group;
SELECT p.ID, l.`T-CHO` FROM Patient p INNER JOIN Laboratory l ON p.ID = l.ID WHERE l.`T-CHO` IS NOT NULL ORDER BY p.Birthday ASC, l.`T-CHO` DESC LIMIT 1;
WITH c_rows AS ( SELECT p.ID, p.SEX, p.Birthday, l.`T-CHO` FROM Patient p INNER JOIN Laboratory l ON p.ID = l.ID WHERE l.`T-CHO` >= 250 AND p.SEX = 'M' ), c_age AS ( SELECT p.ID, p.Birthday, CAST(strftime('%Y', 'now') - strftime('%Y', p.Birthday) AS INTEGER) AS Age FROM c_rows p ) SELECT AVG(Age) AS average_age FROM c_age;
SELECT DISTINCT p.`ID`, p.`Diagnosis` FROM Patient AS p INNER JOIN Laboratory AS l ON p.`ID` = l.`ID` WHERE l.`TG` > 300;
WITH t_final_answer AS ( SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT l.`ID`) AS answer FROM Laboratory AS l INNER JOIN Patient AS p ON l.ID = p.ID WHERE l.`TG` >= 200 AND SUBSTR(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, 1, 4) - SUBSTR(p.`Birthday`, 1, 4) > 50 ) SELECT * FROM t_final_answer;
WITH c_distinct_ids AS ( SELECT DISTINCT l.`ID` FROM Laboratory AS l JOIN Patient AS p ON l.`ID` = p.`ID` WHERE l.`CPK` < 250 AND p.`Admission` = '-' ) SELECT `ID` FROM c_distinct_ids;
SELECT COUNT(*) AS answer FROM ( SELECT DISTINCT p.ID FROM Patient p INNER JOIN Laboratory l ON p.ID = l.ID WHERE p.SEX = 'M' AND strftime('%Y', p.Birthday) BETWEEN '1936' AND '1956' AND l.CPK >= 250 );
WITH t_age_final AS ( SELECT p.`ID`, p.`SEX`, p.`Birthday`, l.`GLU`, l.`T-CHO`, SUBSTR(p.`Birthday`, 1, 4) AS birth_year, SUBSTR(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, 1, 4) - SUBSTR(p.`Birthday`, 1, 4) AS age FROM Patient AS p INNER JOIN Laboratory AS l ON p.`ID` = l.`ID` WHERE l.`GLU` >= 180 AND l.`T-CHO` < 250 ) SELECT `ID`, `SEX`, `age` FROM t_age_final;
SELECT p.ID, l.GLU FROM Patient p JOIN Laboratory l ON p.ID = l.ID WHERE STRFTIME('%Y', p.`First Date`) = '1991' AND l.GLU < 180;
WITH t_final AS ( SELECT p.`ID`, p.`SEX`, p.`Birthday` FROM Patient AS p INNER JOIN Laboratory AS l ON p.`ID` = l.`ID` WHERE l.`WBC` <= 3.5 OR l.`WBC` >= 9.0 ) SELECT `ID`, `SEX`, `Birthday` FROM t_final ORDER BY `SEX`, `Birthday`;
SELECT DISTINCT p.ID, p.`Diagnosis`, CAST(SUBSTR(CURRENT_DATE, 1, 4) AS INTEGER) - CAST(SUBSTR(p.`Birthday`, 1, 4) AS INTEGER) AS age FROM Patient p INNER JOIN Laboratory l ON p.ID = l.ID WHERE l.`RBC` < 3.5;
WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT p.ID, p.SEX, p.Birthday, l.RBC, p.Admission FROM Patient p INNER JOIN Laboratory l ON p.ID = l.ID ), c_rows AS ( SELECT ID, SEX, Birthday, RBC, Admission FROM c_cols WHERE SEX = 'F' AND (SUBSTR('2023', 1, 4) - SUBSTR(Birthday, 1, 4)) >= 50 AND (RBC <= 3.5 OR RBC >= 6.0) ), t_final AS ( SELECT Admission FROM c_rows WHERE Admission = '+' ) SELECT COUNT(*) AS answer FROM t_final;
SELECT DISTINCT p.ID, p.SEX FROM Patient p INNER JOIN Laboratory l ON p.ID = l.ID WHERE p.Admission = '-' AND l.HGB < 10;
SELECT p.ID, p.SEX FROM Patient p JOIN Laboratory l ON p.ID = l.ID WHERE p.Diagnosis = 'SLE' AND l.HGB > 10 AND l.HGB < 17 ORDER BY p.Birthday ASC LIMIT 1;
WITH final_result AS ( SELECT DISTINCT p.ID, (strftime('%Y', 'now') - strftime('%Y', p.Birthday)) AS age FROM Patient p JOIN Laboratory l ON p.ID = l.ID WHERE l.HCT >= 52 AND p.ID IN ( SELECT ID FROM Laboratory GROUP BY ID HAVING COUNT(*) >= 2 ) ) SELECT ID, age FROM final_result;
SELECT AVG(`HCT`) AS answer FROM Laboratory WHERE `Date` LIKE '1991%' AND `HCT` < 29;
WITH t_join AS ( SELECT l.ID, l.PLT FROM Laboratory AS l INNER JOIN Patient AS p ON l.ID = p.ID ), t_lower_count AS ( SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT l.ID) AS lower_count FROM t_join l WHERE l.PLT < 100 ), t_upper_count AS ( SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT l.ID) AS upper_count FROM t_join l WHERE l.PLT > 400 ) SELECT lower_count, upper_count FROM t_lower_count, t_upper_count;
SELECT p.ID, p.`Birthday`, l.`PLT` FROM Patient AS p INNER JOIN Laboratory AS l ON p.ID = l.ID WHERE SUBSTR(l.`Date`, 1, 4) = '1984' AND (SUBSTR(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, 1, 4) - SUBSTR(p.`Birthday`, 1, 4)) < 50 AND l.`PLT` BETWEEN 100 AND 400;
SELECT (SUM(CASE WHEN p.SEX = 'F' AND l.PT >= 14 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) * 100.0 / SUM(CASE WHEN l.PT >= 14 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)) AS percentage FROM Patient p JOIN Laboratory l ON p.ID = l.ID WHERE SUBSTR(p.Birthday, 1, 4) <= DATE('now', '-55 years') AND l.PT IS NOT NULL;
SELECT DISTINCT p.ID FROM Patient AS p INNER JOIN Laboratory AS l ON p.ID = l.ID WHERE strftime('%Y', p.`First Date`) > '1992' AND l.`PT` < 14;

WITH c_rows AS ( SELECT e.ID, e.Symptoms, l.IGG FROM Examination AS e INNER JOIN Patient AS p ON e.ID = p.ID INNER JOIN Laboratory AS l ON e.ID = l.ID WHERE l.IGG > 900 AND l.IGG < 2000 ), t_rows AS ( SELECT ID, Symptoms FROM c_rows WHERE Symptoms IS NOT NULL ) SELECT COUNT(*) AS answer FROM t_rows;

SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS answer FROM Patient p JOIN Laboratory l ON p.ID = l.ID WHERE l.IGG > 900 AND l.IGG < 2000 AND p.Admission = '+';
WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT p.ID, l.GOT, p.Diagnosis FROM Patient p INNER JOIN Laboratory l ON p.ID = l.ID ), c_rows AS ( SELECT ID, GOT, Diagnosis FROM c_cols WHERE GOT >= 60 AND Diagnosis = 'SLE' ), t_rows AS ( SELECT COUNT(*) AS total_with_abnormal_got FROM Laboratory WHERE GOT >= 60 ), final_count AS ( SELECT COUNT(*) AS count_sle_abnormal_got FROM c_rows ) SELECT 100.0 * count_sle_abnormal_got / total_with_abnormal_got AS percentage FROM final_count, t_rows;

WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT p.`ID`, p.`Birthday`, l.`GPT` FROM Patient AS p INNER JOIN Laboratory AS l ON p.`ID` = l.`ID` ), c_rows AS ( SELECT `ID`, `Birthday` FROM c_cols WHERE `GPT` IS NOT NULL AND `GPT` < 60 ), t_join AS ( SELECT r.`ID`, r.`Birthday` FROM c_rows r INNER JOIN Laboratory l ON r.`ID` = l.`ID` WHERE l.`GPT` IS NOT NULL AND l.`GPT` < 60 ), t_agg AS ( SELECT `Birthday`, COUNT(*) AS cnt FROM t_join GROUP BY `Birthday` ORDER BY cnt DESC LIMIT 3 ) SELECT `Birthday` FROM t_agg;

WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT p.`ID`, p.`Birthday`, l.`ALB` FROM Patient AS p INNER JOIN Laboratory AS l ON p.`ID` = l.`ID` ), c_rows AS ( SELECT ID, `ALB` FROM c_cols WHERE `ALB` IS NOT NULL AND `ALB` > 3.5 AND `ALB` < 5.5 ), t_join AS ( SELECT e.`ID`, e.`Examination Date` FROM c_rows r INNER JOIN Examination AS e ON r.ID = e.ID ), t_distinct AS ( SELECT DISTINCT ID, `Examination Date` FROM t_join ), t_max AS ( SELECT ID, MAX(`Examination Date`) AS `Examination Date` FROM t_distinct GROUP BY ID ) SELECT `Examination Date` FROM t_max ORDER BY `Examination Date` DESC LIMIT 1;
WITH final_answer AS ( SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS answer FROM Patient AS p INNER JOIN Laboratory AS l ON p.ID = l.ID WHERE p.SEX = 'M' AND l.ALB > 3.5 AND l.ALB < 5.5 AND l.TP > 6.0 AND l.TP < 8.5 ) SELECT * FROM final_answer;
WITH c_rows AS ( SELECT p.ID, l.UA FROM Patient p JOIN Laboratory l ON p.ID = l.ID WHERE p.SEX = 'F' AND l.UA > 6.50 ), t_join AS ( SELECT l.ID, e.`aCL IgG`, e.`aCL IgM`, e.`aCL IgA` FROM c_rows r JOIN Examination e ON r.ID = e.ID JOIN Laboratory l ON r.ID = l.ID ), t_agg AS ( SELECT ID, MAX(`aCL IgG`) AS max_aCL_IgG, MAX(`aCL IgM`) AS max_aCL_IgM, MAX(`aCL IgA`) AS max_aCL_IgA FROM t_join GROUP BY ID ), t_filter AS ( SELECT ID, max_aCL_IgG, max_aCL_IgM, max_aCL_IgA FROM t_agg ORDER BY max_aCL_IgA DESC LIMIT 1 ) SELECT max_aCL_IgG, max_aCL_IgM, max_aCL_IgA FROM t_filter;

SELECT DISTINCT m.first_name, m.last_name, m.phone FROM member m JOIN expense e ON m.member_id = e.link_to_member WHERE e.cost > (SELECT AVG(cost) FROM expense);
WITH c_rows AS ( SELECT m.member_id, m.position, z.state FROM member AS m JOIN zip_code AS z ON m.zip = z.zip_code WHERE m.position = 'Member' ), t_state_count AS ( SELECT SUM(CASE WHEN state = 'New Jersey' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS nj_count, SUM(CASE WHEN state = 'Vermont' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS vt_count, COUNT(*) AS total_members FROM c_rows ), t_percentage AS ( SELECT nj_count * 100.0 / total_members AS nj_percentage, vt_count * 100.0 / total_members AS vt_percentage FROM t_state_count ), t_diff AS ( SELECT nj_percentage - vt_percentage AS difference FROM t_percentage ) SELECT * FROM t_diff;

WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT m.last_name, m.phone FROM member AS m INNER JOIN major AS mj ON m.link_to_major = mj.major_id WHERE mj.major_name = 'Elementary Education' ) SELECT last_name, phone FROM c_cols;

SELECT m.first_name, m.last_name, i.amount FROM member m INNER JOIN income i ON m.member_id = i.link_to_member WHERE DATE(i.date_received) = '2019-09-09';
SELECT b.category FROM expense e INNER JOIN budget b ON e.link_to_budget = b.budget_id WHERE e.expense_description = 'Posters';
SELECT m.first_name, m.last_name, maj.college FROM member AS m JOIN major AS maj ON m.link_to_major = maj.major_id WHERE m.position = 'Secretary';
SELECT event_name, SUM(spent) AS total_amount FROM budget JOIN event ON budget.link_to_event = event.event_id WHERE budget.category = 'Speaker Gifts' GROUP BY event_name;

SELECT COUNT(*) AS answer FROM ( SELECT `GasStationID` FROM gasstations INNER JOIN products ON gasstations.Segment = products.Description WHERE gasstations.Country = 'CZE' AND products.Description = 'Premium' ) AS c_rows;
SELECT (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM customers WHERE `Currency` = 'EUR') * 1.0 / (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM customers WHERE `Currency` = 'CZK') AS ratio;
WITH final_cte AS ( SELECT ym.`CustomerID`, MIN(ym.`Consumption`) AS min_consumption FROM yearmonth AS ym INNER JOIN customers AS c ON ym.`CustomerID` = c.`CustomerID` INNER JOIN transactions_1k AS t ON ym.`CustomerID` = t.`CustomerID` WHERE SUBSTR(t.`Date`, 1, 4) = '2012' AND c.`Segment` = 'LAM' GROUP BY ym.`CustomerID` ) SELECT `CustomerID`, `min_consumption` FROM final_cte ORDER BY `min_consumption` ASC LIMIT 1;
SELECT AVG(`Consumption`) / 12 AS average_monthly_consumption FROM yearmonth INNER JOIN customers ON yearmonth.`CustomerID` = customers.`CustomerID` WHERE customers.`Segment` = 'SME' AND SUBSTR(yearmonth.`Date`, 1, 4) = '2013';
WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT y.`CustomerID`, y.`Consumption`, c.`Currency` FROM yearmonth y JOIN customers c ON y.`CustomerID` = c.`CustomerID` WHERE c.`Currency` = 'CZK' AND y.`Date` BETWEEN '201101' AND '201112' ) SELECT `CustomerID` FROM ( SELECT `CustomerID`, SUM(`Consumption`) AS total_consumption FROM c_cols GROUP BY `CustomerID` ORDER BY total_consumption DESC LIMIT 1 );
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT ym.CustomerID) AS answer FROM yearmonth AS ym INNER JOIN customers AS c ON ym.CustomerID = c.CustomerID WHERE c.`Segment` = 'KAM' AND ym.Date BETWEEN '201201' AND '201212' AND ym.Consumption < 30000;
SELECT (SUM(CASE WHEN c.`Currency` LIKE '%CZK%' THEN ym.`Consumption` ELSE 0 END) - SUM(CASE WHEN c.`Currency` LIKE '%EUR%' THEN ym.`Consumption` ELSE 0 END)) AS `Difference` FROM `customers` AS c INNER JOIN `yearmonth` AS ym ON c.`CustomerID` = ym.`CustomerID` WHERE substr(ym.`Date`, 1, 4) = '2012' AND (c.`Currency` LIKE '%CZK%' OR c.`Currency` LIKE '%EUR%');
WITH t_join AS ( SELECT ym.`Date`, SUM(ym.`Consumption`) AS total_consumption FROM yearmonth ym JOIN customers c ON ym.CustomerID = c.CustomerID WHERE c.Currency = 'EUR' GROUP BY ym.`Date` ), t_filtered AS ( SELECT `Date`, total_consumption FROM t_join WHERE total_consumption IS NOT NULL ), final_answer AS ( SELECT `Date`, MAX(total_consumption) AS max_consumption FROM t_filtered GROUP BY `Date` ORDER BY max_consumption DESC LIMIT 1 ) SELECT SUBSTR(`Date`, 1, 4) AS Year FROM final_answer;
SELECT customers.Segment, SUM(yearmonth.Consumption) AS TotalConsumption FROM customers JOIN yearmonth ON customers.CustomerID = yearmonth.CustomerID GROUP BY customers.Segment ORDER BY TotalConsumption ASC LIMIT 1;
SELECT SUBSTR(Date, 1, 4) AS Year, SUM(Consumption) AS TotalConsumption FROM yearmonth GROUP BY SUBSTR(Date, 1, 4) ORDER BY TotalConsumption DESC LIMIT 1;
WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT ym.`CustomerID`, ym.`Date`, ym.`Consumption` FROM yearmonth AS ym INNER JOIN customers AS c ON ym.`CustomerID` = c.`CustomerID` WHERE c.`Segment` = 'SME' ), c_rows AS ( SELECT `CustomerID`, `Date`, `Consumption` FROM c_cols WHERE `Date` BETWEEN '201301' AND '201312' ), t_join AS ( SELECT `Date`, `Consumption` FROM c_rows ORDER BY `Consumption` DESC LIMIT 1 ), t_month AS ( SELECT SUBSTR(`Date`, 1, 4) || SUBSTR(`Date`, 6, 2) AS `Month`, `Consumption` FROM t_join ) SELECT `Month` AS answer FROM t_month;
WITH c_segment_avg AS ( SELECT c.`Segment`, SUM(ym.`Consumption`) / COUNT(DISTINCT ym.`CustomerID`) AS annual_avg_consumption FROM yearmonth ym INNER JOIN customers c ON ym.`CustomerID` = c.`CustomerID` WHERE ym.`Date` LIKE '%2013%' GROUP BY c.`Segment` ), c_segment_avg_diff AS ( SELECT 'SME' AS segment1, 'LAM' AS segment2, c1.annual_avg_consumption - c2.annual_avg_consumption AS diff_sme_lam FROM c_segment_avg c1 JOIN c_segment_avg c2 ON c1.Segment = 'SME' AND c2.Segment = 'LAM' UNION ALL SELECT 'LAM' AS segment1, 'KAM' AS segment2, c1.annual_avg_consumption - c2.annual_avg_consumption AS diff_lam_kam FROM c_segment_avg c1 JOIN c_segment_avg c2 ON c1.Segment = 'LAM' AND c2.Segment = 'KAM' UNION ALL SELECT 'KAM' AS segment1, 'SME' AS segment2, c1.annual_avg_consumption - c2.annual_avg_consumption AS diff_kam_sme FROM c_segment_avg c1 JOIN c_segment_avg c2 ON c1.Segment = 'KAM' AND c2.Segment = 'SME' ), final_result AS ( SELECT segment1, segment2, diff_sme_lam AS difference FROM c_segment_avg_diff ) SELECT * FROM final_result;
WITH seg_consumption AS ( SELECT c.Segment, SUM(CASE WHEN ym.Date LIKE '2012%' THEN ym.Consumption END) AS Consumption_2012, SUM(CASE WHEN ym.Date LIKE '2013%' THEN ym.Consumption END) AS Consumption_2013 FROM yearmonth AS ym JOIN customers AS c ON ym.CustomerID = c.CustomerID WHERE ym.Date LIKE '2012%' OR ym.Date LIKE '2013%' AND c.Segment IN ('SME', 'LAM', 'KAM') GROUP BY c.Segment ) SELECT Segment, ROUND((Consumption_2013 - Consumption_2012) * 100.0 / Consumption_2013, 2) AS PercentageIncrease FROM seg_consumption ORDER BY PercentageIncrease DESC;
SELECT SUM(`Consumption`) AS total_consumption FROM yearmonth WHERE `CustomerID` = 6 AND `Date` BETWEEN '201308' AND '201311';
SELECT (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM gasstations WHERE `Country` = 'CZE' AND `Segment` = 'Value for money') - (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM gasstations WHERE `Country` = 'SVK' AND `Segment` = 'Value for money') AS answer;
WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT `CustomerID`, `Date`, `Consumption` FROM yearmonth ), c_rows AS ( SELECT `CustomerID`, `Consumption` FROM c_cols WHERE `Date` = '201304' ), t_join AS ( SELECT t1.`CustomerID`, t1.`Consumption` AS consumption_7, t2.`Consumption` AS consumption_5 FROM c_rows t1 JOIN c_rows t2 ON t1.`CustomerID` = 7 AND t2.`CustomerID` = 5 ) SELECT consumption_7 - consumption_5 AS difference FROM t_join;
WITH c_segment_filtered AS ( SELECT `CustomerID`, `Currency` FROM customers WHERE `Segment` = 'SME' ), c_currency_counts AS ( SELECT SUM(CASE WHEN `Currency` = 'CZK' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS czk_count, SUM(CASE WHEN `Currency` = 'EUR' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS eur_count FROM c_segment_filtered ) SELECT czk_count - eur_count AS difference FROM c_currency_counts;
WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT ym.CustomerID, ym.Date, ym.Consumption, c.Currency, c.Segment FROM yearmonth ym INNER JOIN customers c ON ym.CustomerID = c.CustomerID WHERE ym.Date = '201310' AND c.Segment = 'LAM' ), c_rows AS ( SELECT CustomerID, Consumption FROM c_cols WHERE Currency = 'EUR' ), t_agg AS ( SELECT CustomerID, MAX(Consumption) AS MaxConsumption FROM c_rows GROUP BY CustomerID ), t_final AS ( SELECT CustomerID, MaxConsumption FROM t_agg ORDER BY MaxConsumption DESC LIMIT 1 ) SELECT CustomerID FROM t_final;
WITH t_filtered AS ( SELECT y.`CustomerID`, SUM(y.`Consumption`) AS `Total Consumption` FROM yearmonth AS y INNER JOIN customers AS c ON y.`CustomerID` = c.`CustomerID` WHERE c.`Segment` = 'KAM' GROUP BY y.`CustomerID` ) SELECT `CustomerID`, `Total Consumption` FROM t_filtered ORDER BY `Total Consumption` DESC LIMIT 1;
WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT y.`CustomerID`, y.`Consumption` FROM yearmonth AS y INNER JOIN customers AS c ON y.`CustomerID` = c.`CustomerID` WHERE c.`Segment` = 'KAM' AND y.`Date` = '201305' ), c_rows AS ( SELECT `CustomerID`, SUM(`Consumption`) AS total_consumption FROM c_cols GROUP BY `CustomerID` ) SELECT SUM(total_consumption) AS total_consumption FROM c_rows;
SELECT ( (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT y2.`CustomerID`) FROM yearmonth y2 JOIN customers c2 ON y2.`CustomerID` = c2.`CustomerID` WHERE c2.`Segment` = 'LAM' AND y2.`Consumption` > 46.73) * 100.0 / (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT `CustomerID`) FROM customers WHERE `Segment` = 'LAM') ) AS percentage;
WITH fuzzy_match AS ( SELECT `Country`, `GasStationID` FROM gasstations WHERE `Segment` LIKE '%value%' AND `Segment` LIKE '%money%' ), t_countries AS ( SELECT `Country`, COUNT(`GasStationID`) AS `value_for_money_count` FROM fuzzy_match GROUP BY `Country` ) SELECT `Country`, `value_for_money_count` FROM t_countries ORDER BY `value_for_money_count` DESC;
WITH t_count AS ( SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT c.`CustomerID`) AS total_kam, COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN c.`Currency` = 'EUR' THEN c.`CustomerID` END) AS kam_eur FROM customers AS c JOIN yearmonth AS y ON c.`CustomerID` = y.`CustomerID` WHERE c.`Segment` = 'KAM' ) SELECT (kam_eur * 100.0 / total_kam) AS percentage FROM t_count;

WITH final_answer AS ( SELECT (COUNT(DISTINCT t_premium.`GasStationID`) * 100.0 / COUNT(*) * 1.0) AS percentage FROM ( SELECT `GasStationID` FROM ( SELECT `GasStationID`, `Country`, `Segment` FROM gasstations WHERE `Segment` LIKE '%premium%' OR `Segment` LIKE '%prem%' OR `Segment` LIKE '%pre%' ) AS fuzzy_other ) AS t_premium JOIN gasstations AS c_cols ON t_premium.`GasStationID` = c_cols.`GasStationID` WHERE c_cols.`Country` LIKE '%Slovakia%' OR c_cols.`Country` LIKE '%Slovak' OR c_cols.`Country` LIKE '%Slov%' ) SELECT * FROM final_answer;

SELECT c.`Segment` FROM yearmonth y JOIN customers c ON y.`CustomerID` = c.`CustomerID` WHERE y.`Date` = '201208' AND y.`Consumption` = 548.4;
SELECT (COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN c.`Currency` = 'EUR' THEN t.`CustomerID` END) * 100.0 / COUNT(DISTINCT t.`CustomerID`)) AS percentage FROM transactions_1k t JOIN customers c ON t.`CustomerID` = c.`CustomerID` WHERE t.`Date` = '2012-08-25';
WITH t_consumption_2012 AS ( SELECT ym.CustomerID, ym.Consumption AS consumption_2012 FROM yearmonth AS ym INNER JOIN customers AS c ON ym.CustomerID = c.CustomerID WHERE ym.Date = '201208' ), t_consumption_2013 AS ( SELECT ym.CustomerID, ym.Consumption AS consumption_2013 FROM yearmonth AS ym INNER JOIN customers AS c ON ym.CustomerID = c.CustomerID WHERE ym.Date = '201308' ), t_merged AS ( SELECT t2012.CustomerID, t2012.consumption_2012, t2013.consumption_2013 FROM t_consumption_2012 t2012 LEFT JOIN t_consumption_2013 t2013 ON t2012.CustomerID = t2013.CustomerID ) SELECT (consumption_2012 - consumption_2013) / consumption_2012 AS consumption_decrease_rate FROM t_merged WHERE consumption_2012 LIKE '%634.8%';
WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT t.`GasStationID`, SUM(t.`Amount`) AS TotalRevenue FROM transactions_1k AS t GROUP BY t.`GasStationID` ), c_rows AS ( SELECT `GasStationID`, `TotalRevenue` FROM c_cols ORDER BY `TotalRevenue` DESC LIMIT 1 ), t_join AS ( SELECT g.`GasStationID`, g.`ChainID`, g.`Country`, g.`Segment`, c.`TotalRevenue` FROM gasstations AS g INNER JOIN c_rows c ON g.`GasStationID` = c.`GasStationID` ) SELECT `GasStationID`, `ChainID`, `Country`, `Segment`, `TotalRevenue` FROM t_join;

WITH total_spent AS ( SELECT SUM(`Consumption`) AS total_spent FROM yearmonth WHERE `CustomerID` = '38508' ), jan_2012_spent AS ( SELECT SUM(`Consumption`) AS jan_2012_spent FROM yearmonth WHERE `CustomerID` = '38508' AND `Date` = '201201' ) SELECT * FROM total_spent, jan_2012_spent;
WITH t_join AS ( SELECT t.`ProductID`, p.`Description`, SUM(t.`Amount`) AS TotalSales FROM transactions_1k AS t JOIN products AS p ON t.`ProductID` = p.`ProductID` GROUP BY t.`ProductID`, p.`Description` ), t_sorted AS ( SELECT `ProductID`, `Description`, `TotalSales` FROM t_join ORDER BY `TotalSales` DESC LIMIT 5 ) SELECT `Description` FROM t_sorted;
WITH c_cols AS ( SELECT t.`CustomerID`, t.`Price`, t.`Amount`, c.`Currency` FROM transactions_1k t INNER JOIN customers c ON t.`CustomerID` = c.`CustomerID` ), c_rows AS ( SELECT `CustomerID`, SUM(`Price`) AS total_price, SUM(`Amount`) AS total_amount, `Currency` FROM c_cols GROUP BY `CustomerID`, `Currency` ), t_join AS ( SELECT r.`CustomerID`, r.total_price, r.total_amount, r.`Currency` FROM c_rows r INNER JOIN customers c ON r.`CustomerID` = c.`CustomerID` ), t_avg AS ( SELECT `CustomerID`, `Currency`, total_price / total_amount AS avg_price_per_item FROM t_join ), final_answer AS ( SELECT `CustomerID`, `Currency`, `avg_price_per_item` FROM t_avg ORDER BY `avg_price_per_item` DESC LIMIT 1 ) SELECT * FROM final_answer;
WITH country_max_prices AS ( SELECT g.`Country`, MAX(t.`Price`) AS max_price FROM transactions_1k t INNER JOIN gasstations g ON t.`GasStationID` = g.`GasStationID` WHERE t.`ProductID` = 2 GROUP BY g.`Country` ), overall_max_price AS ( SELECT MAX(max_price) AS global_max FROM country_max_prices ) SELECT c.`Country` FROM country_max_prices c INNER JOIN overall_max_price o ON c.max_price = o.global_max LIMIT 1;
